    ["Scale salary: The employer is obliged to pay the employee the scale salary based on his job classification. The employee's scale salary upon commencement of employment is set at a minimum in the A-periodical (job group 2) or 0 periodical (job group 3 or higher) of the relevant salary scale."],
    ["Employee without required knowledge/skill/experience: The employee who does not yet possess the knowledge, skill, and/or experience required for the fulfillment of a position can receive the scale salary for a maximum of 12 months in accordance with the next lower job group in which this position is classified. After 12 months, the employee will be classified at the next higher scale salary of the job group belonging to his position."],
    ["Job group 2 entry salary: For the employee who commences employment or works as a temporary worker in a position classified in job group 2, his scale salary upon commencement of employment or the start of the temporary employment period can be set at a maximum of 26 weeks in the A-periodical. The maximum of 26 weeks applies to all combined periods that the employee has been employed by an employer as referred to in Article 1 paragraph 2 or has worked as a temporary worker for an employer as referred to in Article 1 paragraph 2. The burden of proof for this lies with the employee."],
    ["Holiday worker remuneration: The holiday worker can be paid according to the statutory minimum (youth) wage for a maximum of 1 month on an annual basis. After this, the holiday worker will be paid according to the CAO scale salary of the job group in which his position is classified."],
    ["Acting position allowance: The employee who, at the employer's instruction, temporarily fully performs a position classified higher than his own position, remains classified in his own job group. If the acting position has lasted for at least 5 consecutive shifts, the employee receives an allowance on his scale salary for the entire duration of the acting position. This allowance amounts to 1/20th of the difference between the scale salaries per period of the respective job groups at step 0 per full shift. Any classification allowance will be offset against the acting allowance, as stated in the transitional arrangement. The shift allowance and the overtime allowance must be calculated on the temporarily increased scale salary, where applicable. The acting allowance is not granted to the employee for whom the temporary performance of a position has already been taken into account in the classification of his position."],
    ["Scale salary revision: Each year on January 1, the employee is granted a periodical increase until he has reached the maximum of his job group and the employment has lasted at least 22 weeks."],
    ["Promotion salary increase: Upon promotion of an employee to a higher job group, the increase in his scale salary amounts to once the difference between the functional wages at the 0-periodicals of the old and new job group, or as much more as necessary to bring the new scale salary in line with the next higher amount in the periodical scale of the new job group. The employee is then granted the number of periodical increases corresponding to the amount thus found, and the next periodical increase each subsequent January 1."],
    ["Demotion salary adjustment: Upon classification in a lower job group at the employee's request or due to the employee's own actions, the new scale salary can be equated to the next lower scale salary in the new job group. This reduction is applied as of the first day of the payment period following that in which the placement in a lower job group took place."],
    ["Demotion due to business circumstances: If no equivalent position is available, the employee is classified in a new lower job group. In that case, the new scale salary is equal to the next higher scale salary in the new job group. If the old scale salary is above the maximum of his new job group, the difference between the old scale salary and this maximum is converted into a personal allowance. The personal allowance is increased by the percentage of the general initial salary increase. Upon reclassification into a higher job group, the personal allowance is reduced by the same amount as the salary or scale salary increases. This change of the scale salary is applied as of the first day of the payment period following that in which the placement in a lower job group took place."],
    ["Guaranteed salary for older employees: If an employee aged 50 or older with an employment of 2 years or longer is classified in a lower job group, the applicable scale salary belonging to the (higher) job group before reclassification will be guaranteed. Of both parties, maximum effort is expected to function in the existing position. This guarantee does not apply if the transfer takes place at the employee's own request."],
    ["Performance-dependent remuneration system: The purpose of the performance-dependent remuneration system is to more explicitly establish a relationship between performance, development, and remuneration. The system is aimed at the development and performance of employees in such a way that a positive outcome affects the individual salary increase of the employee concerned. In addition, the system provides tools for job- and career-oriented development of employees within the confectionery industry. The application of the performance-dependent remuneration system is not mandatory. It is intended for companies that see this as a good opportunity to work on employee development in a targeted manner, in order to better respond to future process and product innovations. If a company decides to use the system, a number of conditions must be met: submitting a total overview of the assessments to the Appeals Committee for the Confectionery Industry; an employee can never be confronted with the non-allocation of a salary step for more than two consecutive years; the assessment must take place before January 1; presence of an appeal procedure according to a fixed model. In Appendix XI of the CAO, the framework conditions are further elaborated. The remuneration system consists of a combination of the existing automatic periodical system and remuneration based on assessments. The new remuneration system has a starting salary line (0-scale) and a maximum line. The first 3 steps are increased based on the automatic step system. After the guarantee line, the increase depends on the employee's assessment based on an assessment system. Employees who have reached the maximum salary line receive a bonus of 2.5% of their scale salary for an excellent assessment. For the setup of the assessment system, a three-point scale will be used: A-score = Insufficient (No salary step), B-score = Normal (1 salary step), C-score = Excellent (2 salary steps or bonus)."],
    ["CAO à la Carte: In consultation with the works council, or, in the absence of this body, in consultation with the employee representation or the staff, a choice model for employment conditions can be established. Sources are employment conditions that can be brought into the choice model for employment conditions to be exchanged for other employment conditions. Goals are employment conditions that can be realized by using the aforementioned sources. Sources and goals represent an entitlement in time or in money. The employment conditions to be exchanged are: Sources: Extra-statutory vacation days, Gross wage, Extra free age-related hours (Article 35), Extra free hours for night work (Article 36). Goals: Extra free time with a maximum of 10 vacation days per year (if current vacation days are insufficient), Saving vacation days for long-term leave or sustainable employability, Reimbursement of study costs (if tax-exempt), Personal budget for sustainable employability (training, care tasks, financial scan, other DIM-stimulating initiatives), Gross wage. In addition to the above-mentioned monetary and time entitlements, other matters can be designated as sources and/or goals at the initiative of employers and employees. In that case, these sources and/or goals must be submitted to the Appeals Committee for approval in advance. Gross wage paid under this choice scheme will be paid subject to payroll taxes. Conditions: Registration of the entitlements built up by this system takes place in money. At the time the employee makes the choice for spending in time, the entitlements in money are converted into the value in time at that moment. For the application of the choice model for employment conditions, the value of a day is determined at 1/261st of the income as mentioned in Article 1, paragraph 10 on an annual basis. The purchase or sale of vacation days does not affect the basis of the vacation allowance and other wage-dependent employment conditions such as the hourly wage and pension. If an employee uses the choice model, any consequences in the area of taxes, social insurance, and pension are for the account of the employee. Extra free age-related hours (Article 35) and extra free hours for night work (Article 36) cannot be exchanged for gross wages. If the employer does not offer an 'à la Carte' scheme, the employee can buy 10 vacation days per year if the vacation balance in the current calendar year is insufficient. The value of a day is calculated in accordance with the provisions in paragraph 3 sub c. Purchased days off cannot be carried over to subsequent years or used for purposes other than enjoying vacation. If, by December 31 of the year in which the vacation days were purchased, it appears that not all days and/or hours have been enjoyed, the remaining hours will be paid out based on the same wage level as they were purchased for."],
    ["Clock Hour Matrix Allowance (Article 19, paragraph 1)", "Time Range, Monday, Tuesday, Wednesday, Thursday, Friday, Saturday, Sunday", "00:00 - 03:00, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 110%", "03:00 - 06:30, 39%, 39%, 39%, 39%, 39%, 39%, 110%", "06:30 - 07:30, -, -, -, -, -, 39%, 110%", "07:30 - 15:00, -, -, -, -, -, 50%, 110%", "15:00 - 18:00, -, -, -, -, -, 83%, 110%", "18:00 - 22:00, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 83%, 110%", "22:00 - 24:00, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 38%, 110%, 38%"],
    ["Shift allowance: If an employee has a work schedule in which work is performed outside the day window, the average shift allowance on the salary is calculated based on the attendance hours (these are the paid and unpaid hours between the start and end of the shift) in the shift cycle of the employee concerned. Per shift cycle, the sum of the allowance percentages indicated in Article 19 paragraph 1 for all his attendance hours is determined. This sum is divided by the total number of attendance hours in the shift cycle. This yields the average allowance per hour. Together with the hourly wage, this forms the hourly value. If the total number of working hours in the shift cycle is greater than or equal to the working hours mentioned in Article 8 paragraph 1 (part-time pro rata), the shift allowance is equal to this average allowance. In all other cases, the hourly value is multiplied by a factor equal to the scheduled working hours divided by the contractual working hours. The result of this is the salary including shift allowance. The shift allowance is then this percentage minus 100%. The outcome of the calculation is rounded arithmetically to a multiple of a quarter percent."],
    ["Annual shift allowance: If more than one shift schedule applies to an employee during a calendar year, and work is performed outside the day window in one or more of those schedules, an annual shift allowance (jaarroostertoeslag) will be paid to the employee concerned for that year, based on the weighted average of the shift allowances of those shift schedules, as calculated in accordance with the methodology indicated in paragraph 1. If the pre-calculated annual shift allowance is lower than the actual weighted average of the shift allowances for the different shift schedules, the employee will be paid this difference in allowance. In all other cases, no settlement takes place. Transition from a work schedule with a higher annual shift allowance to a work schedule with a lower (annual) shift allowance in the following year gives the right to the phasing-out scheme in accordance with Article 21 paragraph 2 sub a and b. The reference point for the phasing-out is the (recalculated) annual shift allowance percentage that should have actually been paid in the preceding year."],

  "pension_information": [
    ["Pension scheme details: The employee is obliged to participate in the pension provision of the Stichting Bedrijfspensioenfonds voor de Zoetwarenindustrie unless the fund has granted an exemption to the employer. A scheme for lifelong old-age pension where the pension age is generally 68 years, but the actual commencement date is flexible. The starting point for the premium distribution between employer and employees is a ratio of 2:1 up to 30% (= maximum premium). With a total premium of 30% (2021 level), this leads to a distribution of 20% and 10% respectively. This premium is levied on the pension basis, being the pensionable wage – with a maximum of € 61,809 – minus the franchise of € 14,544 (2021 levels). The accrual in 2021 is 1.7% of the pension basis. As of January 1, 2019, the franchise has been adjusted to the level of the statutory minimum franchise. The maximum pensionable wage is increased annually by the same percentage as the franchise. The possible consequences for the pension premium and the accrual percentage for 2022 will be determined by CAO parties in the Organized Consultation in November. The starting point is maintaining the maximum premium of 30%."],
    ["Gross payment upon (pre)retirement: The employee whose employment with the employer has lasted continuously for 10 or more years receives, when the employment ends in a regular manner due to (pre)retirement or due to reaching the AOW-eligible age, a gross payment from the employer equal to the income over one payment period (i.e., 4 weeks or one month). The payment is made only once."],
    ["Leave for pension preparation: The older employee who attends a course in preparation for his (pre)retirement is entitled to leave with retention of income insofar as his work can be arranged in consultation with the person concerned. This leave is limited to a maximum of 5 shifts during the last 3 years combined."],
    ["Parental leave and pension entitlements: The employee who makes use of parental leave based on the provisions in Article 6:1 of the Work and Care Act retains his pension entitlements."],
    ["RVU scheme research: Parties will investigate in a joint working group how to implement the possibility included in the Pension Agreement (June 2019) to allow employees in demanding professions to retire 3 years prior to the AOW age with a gross payment of € 21,200 per year, in accordance with the so-called RVU scheme. The working group will pay attention to the conditions and criteria for participation in an RVU scheme and its costs and financing. Existing senior schemes such as the 80/90/100 scheme can also be included in the research. The possibilities to use subsidies will be included in the research. The working group can be supported by external experts and aims to present a study result by November 1, 2021 at the latest, both in terms of content and costs and financing. The result will be discussed in the Organized Consultation and included in the CAO as of January 1, 2022."],
    ["WGA gap insurance: For the income shortfall, there is a possibility to take out WGA gap insurance. To this end, a collective contract has been concluded within the industry, which all employees can voluntarily participate in, if this insurance is offered by the employer. This benefit, which guarantees an income of 70% of the last earned wage (up to the annually determined maximum daily wage), will be provided for a maximum period of 10 years. After this 10-year period, a basic benefit follows, which supplements up to 70% x the disability percentage x the last earned income (up to the maximum daily wage)."]

      "Scope of Agreement (Article B-1)",
      "This CAO applies to employees in service of employers if and insofar as the regulations arising from the Whw apply to the activities of these employees. This CAO can be declared applicable to employees who work at a hogeschool whose activities are subject to regulations other than those arising from the Whw. If legal provisions are or become established from which deviation is not permitted, this CAO applies only insofar as it does not conflict with the aforementioned legal provisions. In that case, consultation between CAO parties will follow. Unless otherwise provided therein, the employer is not permitted to deviate from the provisions of this CAO. The employer is permitted to agree on employment conditions in local CAO consultation regarding subjects not regulated in the CAO."
    ],
    [
      "Conflict with Funding Conditions (Article B-2)",
      "In case of conflict between the funding conditions and the decisions based thereon with this CAO, the funding conditions prevail."
    ],
    [
      "Scope of CAO (Article B-4)",
      "Employees in service of a legal entity over which the hogeschool has control, as well as temporary workers and seconded staff, who contribute to the core activities as referred to in Article B-1 paragraph 1 within the hogeschool, are entitled to remuneration in accordance with the CAO-hbo, including allowances and expense reimbursements and the employer's pension contribution, regardless of whether they are skilled workers or not. The employer will make agreements with the temporary employment agency if necessary."
    ],
    [
      "Hardship Clause (Article B-5)",
      "CAO parties will implement the agreements made reasonably and equitably. If and insofar as the implementation of agreements demonstrably threatens the continuity of a hogeschool, additional agreements can be made at hogeschool level in agreement with the trade unions to safeguard continuity."
    ],

      "Application Code, Establishment and Availability (Article C-1)",

    ],
    [
      "Medical Examination (Article C-2)",
      "The Wet op de Medische Keuringen applies. For cases where a medical examination should take place in accordance with the law but the employee has serious conscientious or moral objections, the consequences of illnesses and defects that could reasonably have been detected during an examination and that lead to unsuitability for the fulfillment of the function are at the expense and risk of the employee."
    ],
    [
      "Employment Contract (Article C-3)",
      "The employment contract is entered into and amended in writing in accordance with the model included in Bijlage II. The employment contract is drawn up in duplicate. The employer ensures that the employee receives a copy of this agreement or its amendment, signed by both parties, in a timely manner, if possible before commencing employment. The employee is appointed at the hogeschool or at the legal entity that maintains the hogeschool or the entirety of hogescholen in a function at the hogeschool or at the entirety of hogescholen. The employment contract includes a description of the main lines of the tasks to be performed by the employee, also to improve employability. The starting point is that the employee has an employment contract with the hogeschool, without a strict definition of function and standplaats. Changes in the organization of study programs may alter the function content and the place where the work is usually performed, without adjusting the employment contract and without consequences for the agreed employment conditions. The employer consults with the employee about important aspects of the function content, its alteration, and the consequences of an alteration for, among other things, travel expenses and travel time. The starting point here is that the activities can reasonably be assigned to this employee given their function level."
    ],

      "Part-time Employment (Article D-7)",
      "Every employee has the right to reduce their employment size, unless compelling business or service interests oppose it. Employees with a part-time employment, in case of a vacancy, are given priority to expand their employment size if sufficiently suitable. In the context of this article, there is no vacancy if the formation space can be filled by an employee threatened with dismissal or by a former employee who uses the Bovenwettelijke werkloosheidsregeling hbo. With part-time employees, it is agreed annually in writing at what times they can be deployed."

      "General Obligations (Article E-1)",

The employee is obliged to fulfill their function to the best of their ability and to act in accordance with the instructions given by or on behalf of the employer. The employee is obliged to fulfill their function in accordance with the principles and objectives as described in the statutes of the legal entity that maintains the hogeschool and as described in other documents in which the principles and objectives are laid down. The employee must reasonably have been able to take note of these documents. Within reasonable limits and insofar as necessary for a good continuation of the activities, the employee is obliged to agree, after prior consultation, to: a. a change for a short period, but for a maximum of one month, of the activities associated with their function; b. a change for a short period, but for a maximum of one month, in the arrangement of their working hours. This paragraph applies mutatis mutandis if such special circumstances arise that prior consultation is not possible."
    ],

      "Confidentiality (Article E-2)",

This obligation also applies after the termination of the employment contract. Without prejudice to legal provisions, the employer is obliged towards third parties to maintain confidentiality of personal data of the employee, unless the employee gives written permission for the provision of data relating to their person."
    ],

      "Work Activities (Article E-3)",

    ],

      "Ancillary Activities (Article E-4)",
      "The employee will report the scope and content of ancillary activities related to their work and professionalism. They will not perform activities that demonstrably harm the fulfillment of their function and the interest of the employer. The employee is obliged to transfer compensation from third parties for activities performed by the employee during working hours or compensation for granting work to those third parties, or any other compensation paid by third parties in connection with any relationship with the employment contract with the employer, to the employer, unless the employer has granted them written exemption from this obligation. If the employee has committed to a duty of confidentiality regarding the content through an agreement, they are not obliged to inform the employer about the content of the activities."

The employer's obligation to insure the employee as referred to in the first paragraph does not apply if this employee has serious conscientious or moral objections to the said insurance and has communicated these objections to the employer in writing. If there are serious conscientious or moral objections to an insurance as referred to in the preceding paragraph, the consequences of not being insured are at the expense and risk of the employee. In case of insurance as referred to in the first paragraph, the employer indemnifies the employee against liability in this regard and waives any possibility of recourse against the employee. This is subject to cases where the damage is the result of intent or conscious recklessness on the part of the employee."

The hogeschool provides the possibility for internal appeal. An existing internal appeal procedure can be used, provided that the diligence and independence of the procedure are sufficiently guaranteed. The appeal procedure provides in any case for a regulation for the following situations: An employee who has not been appraised has the right to be appraised upon request. In that case, the immediate superior ensures that an appraisal takes place within three months using the methodology; An employee who cannot agree with the form, content, or outcome of the appraisal can (possibly after a re-appraisal by the immediate superior) lodge an internal appeal against the appraisal. The lodging of an appeal will not affect the date on which the outcome of the appraisal will be processed in a salary adjustment. It will be stipulated that the employee will not suffer any negative consequences from lodging an appeal. The manner in which appraisal interviews are prepared and conducted is part of the system. The employer will elaborate an appraisal system based on these criteria and submit it for approval to the local CAO consultation. The agreement on the appraisal system will be registered by the employer as a CAO agreement with the Minister of SZW, directie Uitvoeringstaken Arbeidsvoorwaardenwetgeving (UAW)."

      "Decentralized Employment Conditions Funds (Article K)",
      "The hogeschool spends 1.41% of the total annual income on decentralized consultation on employment conditions. This budget is available for new agreements to be made by local CAO consultation or for improving existing regulations on, among other things, the following topics: paid parental leave (a regulation in this area will at least imply that during the period of parental leave, the accrual of pension rights will be fully continued and that the basis for social security will not be affected); childcare (a regulation in this area will at least relate to the care of children in the age category 0 to 4 years. The mandatory employer contribution per employee will be deducted from the available budget); collective disability insurance (if no collective disability insurance is offered in the decentralized consultation, the employer will inform employees in writing about the risk of income loss after the wage-related phase and the possibility of insuring this risk); target group policy (policy agreements will be aimed at removing physical and financial barriers to employment for immigrants, disabled persons, people with subsidized jobs, and women); participation policy/jobs (discussion will take place on whether and to what extent participation jobs can be used within the hogeschool. The possibility of flow-through from people with a participation job to regular work will also be examined); supplementary agreements regarding the travel expense scheme for commuting, including the provision of an electric bicycle; supplementary care leave agreements; supplementary health policy agreements; supplementary career policy agreements (for employees who wish to prepare for a function elsewhere on their own initiative); teleworking. Regarding the aforementioned policy topics, the content and level of the regulations in force at institution level on May 31, 2002, whether or not arising from the LKD policy, are starting points for the consultation of CAO parties at local level. Until the effective date of new agreements, the existing regulations apply, whether or not based on previous CAO provisions. In the local CAO consultation, the spending of funds is periodically discussed, and it is determined in consultation whether there is reason for changes in the allocation of funds across the various policy areas. The agreements on the spending of the budget will be registered by the employer as a CAO agreement with the Minister of SZW, directie Uitvoeringstaken Arbeidsvoorwaardenwetgeving (UAW). In the local consultation, CAO parties can agree on a scheme to classify 30% of the CAO salary of a foreign employee as a (tax-free) allowance under certain conditions."

    [
      "Choice Menu Employment Conditions (Article L)",
      "With due observance of the provisions below, a choice menu of employment conditions has been implemented at hogeschool level in consultation with the trade unions. For the choice menu of employment conditions, the following sources and goals apply, whereby sources are understood to mean the employment conditions that the employee can use as a means of exchange for other employment conditions. Both for sources and for goals, a distinction can be made between time and money. Sources in time: the hours to be worked that exceed the agreed normjaartaak. As a rule, these will be the freely available hours above the statutory vacation and roster-free days/hours, with due observance of a maximum of 90 hours per year, with the exception of the situation in which time is directly converted into money. In that case, a maximum of 45 hours per year applies. If time is used for other goals in money, the maximum of 90 hours applies. Hours taken as free time in Article M-1, and the right to work time reduction for seniors from Article M-2, are not included here. Sources in money: one-time payments insofar as included in the CAO; structural end-of-year bonus (from January 1, 2003); salary. Goals in time: sabbatical leave; extension of parental leave; study leave (in case of non-function-related study); extra leave hours up to a maximum of 45 hours per year. Goals in money: income supplement during parental leave; own contribution childcare; reimbursement of study costs (in case of non-function-related study); accrual of old-age pension (OP) based on ABP regulations; accrual of life-course salary in the context of the life-course scheme; money up to a maximum equivalent of 45 hours per year; electric bicycle. Regarding the sources and goals, the following specific provisions apply: taking sabbatical leave preferably occurs once every four years for a period of two months, with a maximum of once every six years for a maximum period of three months; a choice for sabbatical leave remains unchanged for several years; such a provision applies to both the employer and the employee. Nevertheless, circumstances may arise that necessitate deviation from a made and recorded agreement. A revision of the made agreement may therefore be necessary under compelling circumstances. A condition here is that the interests of the service where the employee concerned is employed do not oppose this; in case of premature termination of employment (voluntary or involuntary), the savings balance accumulated in the context of sabbatical leave is equated with the employee's remaining vacation leave; in case of illness during the sabbatical leave period, the employee must immediately report this to the employer. If the period of illness lasts longer than four weeks, the sabbatical leave period is suspended; when cashing in saved hours in a future period, a 1-to-1 system must apply. This will be in accordance with the accrual of leave rights; spending for the goals related to parental leave, childcare, and study can be accumulated over several years; additional contributions on behalf of the employee by the employer for old-age pension (OP) are made in accordance with the provisions of the ABP regulations; the fiscal consequences in wage and income tax on the various sources and/or goals in the choice model are for the account of the employee and will not be compensated by the employer. The value of all elements in the model must be expressible in a standard. 1 hour is chosen as the standard. The valuation of one hour amounts to 1/138th (0.725%) of the monthly salary. The vacation allowance is included in this percentage. For fixed-term employment, the duration of the temporary employment must be taken into account. For employees working part-time, the pro rata principle applies. A request from the employee to buy or sell hours is submitted to the employer before the start of the year (calendar or academic year) and generally applies for a period of 1 year, unless otherwise specified. The choices become final for the coming year after the employer has given written consent. The employer decides on the submitted requests, whereby elements such as fitting within the formation and budgetary consequences will be decisive for approval. The employer can, for business economic reasons, make a collective (meaning an existing business unit within the organization) request to employees to buy or sell hours. The employee can refuse such a request."

      "Agreements between CAO-hbo 2000-2002 parties on the introduction of job classification in higher professional education.     [
      "Standard Functions and Other Functions (Article F-1)",
      "The employer establishes function descriptions and salary scales for the functions distinguished within the formation. The employer indicates the place in the organization and, in conjunction with the formation, determines the salary scale, taking into account the standards applicable to employees based on laws and regulations and the standards applicable to teaching staff. The employer ensures that the functions are sufficiently broad and varied insofar as these functions comprise at least 0.5 of the normbetrekking. An exception to the function classification system is made for activities of students related to the introduction, information, and recruitment of new fellow students. These are remunerated at the minimum of scale 1."
    ],
    [
      "Job Evaluation (Article F-2)",
      "The employee's function is classified into one of the function groups 1 to 18, which are also salary groups, based on the employer's function evaluation system (FUWA-HBO or Hay method). The classification of functions takes place within the ratios of function weight established in the Conversiebestand Hoger Beroepsonderwijs. The summary of the Conversiebestand in the function matrix, in which these ratios are established, is included in Bijlage IX of this CAO. If an academic work and thinking level (master's level) is required when recruiting employees for a vacant function, the formal description of the advertised function substantiates this requirement. When a function requires an academic work and thinking level (master's level), the application of the function evaluation system leads to a function weight corresponding to wage scale 11 or higher. The employee can at any time object to the evaluation of their function. The 'Landelijke bezwarenreglement functieordenen hbo' as included in Bijlage V applies."
    ],
    [
      "Salary Determination (Article H-1)",
      "The employee's salary is agreed upon with due observance of the rules established by CAO parties by or pursuant to laws and regulations. Salaries will be increased by 2.75% as of July 1, 2020. The salary increase will apply to benefits in the usual manner. In June 2020, a one-time gross payment of €880 will be granted, pro-rata to the employment size, to every employee who is employed by the hogeschool on June 1, 2020, falling under the CAO. This amount is pensionable. An employee working part-time is entitled to the payments pro-rata to the part-time factor. In case of fully unpaid leave, not being life-course leave, there is no right to the one-time payment. The employee's salary is reasonably determined upon commencing employment, taking into account accumulated experience, within the salary belonging to the function based on the function classification or within the induction years established for that scale, and is paid monthly. The employer provides the employee with a specification of their earned income upon commencing employment and if a change occurs. The vacation allowance amounts to 8% of the amount the employee received in salary in that month, per calendar month, and is paid once a year in May for the period of twelve months ending in May. Notwithstanding this, in case of termination of employment, the payment takes place for the period between the end of the last period for which the vacation allowance was paid and the date of termination. Regarding the minimum vacation allowance, Bijlage III applies. In December, a structural end-of-year bonus of 8.3% is granted, calculated over the annual salary and accrued pro-rata to the employment size in accordance with the vacation allowance system. This payment affects the basis for pensions and both current and new benefits."

      "Salary for Part-time Workers (Article H-2)",
      "The salary of an employee who commences employment in a part-time position is calculated proportionally to this CAO and the scope of employment."

    [
      "Annual Salary Increase (Article H-3)",
      "The granting of an annual salary increase depends on the outcome of the annual appraisal. An operational appraisal system that meets the criteria agreed upon by CAO parties applies within the institution. These criteria are laid down in Hoofdstuk N of this CAO. With the exception of the situation referred to in paragraph 3, an employee who performs well, meaning at the normal level appropriate to the function, is granted an annual salary increase within the salary scale with the norm percentage applicable to the function scale. The employee is notified thereof. In case the employer deems performance to be excellent, meaning above the normal level appropriate to the function, or insufficient, meaning regularly or continuously below the normal level appropriate to the function, an appraisal interview will be held at the initiative of the manager. The employee has the right to request an appraisal interview. The outcome of the appraisal leads to: an excellent appraisal resulting in a salary increase of twice the norm percentage; an insufficient appraisal resulting in no salary adjustment; a good appraisal resulting in once the norm percentage. In case an employee, due to not yet meeting all requirements appropriate for a full fulfillment of the function, is classified in the induction years trajectory appropriate to the relevant function scale, and the appraisal determines that there is now a full fulfillment of the function, the outcome of the annual appraisal leads to a salary adjustment to at least the minimum of the relevant function scale. The number of induction years is subject to a maximum and amounts to: for scales 1 to 5: no induction years; for scales 6 to 8: maximum one year; for scales 9 to 14: maximum two years; for scales 15 to 18: maximum three years."
    ],
    [
      "Extraordinary Allowance (Article H-4)",
      "The employer can grant an employee who has reached the maximum of the function scale belonging to their function a permanent allowance of up to 15% of their applicable salary based on: a. extraordinary competence, suitability, and diligence; b. other circumstances deemed sufficiently important by the employer."
    ],
    [
      "Gratification (Article H-5)",
      "The employer can grant the employee a one-time gratification for a special achievement. The employer grants the employee a gratification upon reaching a 25-year, 40-year, and 50-year service anniversary, amounting to 50%, 100%, and 100% of their monthly income, respectively. The number of service years valid for ABP pension determines the service anniversary. If the employee has bought off pension-eligible service years or has only partially or not accrued them for a certain period due to their age, employment size, or duration of employment, these years do count fully for the calculation of the service anniversary. The following years do not count for the calculation of the service anniversary: pension years that the employee has bought in; pension years that have been accrued additionally because the employee has an employment size greater than 1 FTE. Years spent in the tropics that are considered double pension years by the pension fund count only once for the calculation of the service anniversary."
    ],
    [
      "Labor Market Allowance (Article H-6)",
      "The employer can grant the employee an allowance based on labor market considerations if the function cannot be filled or is difficult to fill without the said allowance. Agreements on the duration of the allowance are made directly upon entering into the employment contract. The granting of allowances based on labor market considerations is accounted for annually in the social annual report."
    ],
    [
      "Guarantee Allowance for Irregular Service (Article H-9)",
      "An employee whose income, as a result of the involuntary termination or reduction of an allowance as referred to in Article H-8, undergoes a permanent reduction of at least 3% of the income, is granted a declining allowance, provided that they had received the first-mentioned allowance, immediately prior to the time of the aforementioned termination or reduction, for at least two years without significant interruption. The declining allowance as referred to in the first sentence of this paragraph has a duration equal to 25% of the period for which the irregular service allowance as referred to in Article H-8 was granted, with a maximum of three years. The declining allowance amounts, divided over equal parts of the duration for which the declining allowance is granted, respectively 75%, 50%, and 25%. Notwithstanding the first paragraph, an employee aged 60 or older, whose income undergoes a permanent reduction as a result of the involuntary termination or reduction of an allowance referred to in Article H-8, is granted a permanent allowance, provided that they had received the first-mentioned allowance, immediately prior to the time of the aforementioned termination or reduction, for at least ten years without significant interruption. The allowance as referred to in the first sentence of this paragraph amounts to 100%. The declining allowance referred to in the first paragraph becomes a permanent allowance as referred to in the second paragraph of this article when the employee reaches the age of 60 and, immediately prior to the start of that allowance, had received an allowance as referred to in Article H-8 for at least ten years without significant interruption. For the application of the first to third paragraphs, a significant interruption is understood to mean an interruption of longer than two months."
    ],
    [
      "Acting in a Higher Function (Article H-10)",
      "If the employee is assigned the full or partial acting role in a function for which a higher function scale applies, the employee is classified for the duration of that acting role as if they were appointed to that function, unless the acting role: a. belongs to the employee's function; b. results from another employee's vacation leave; c. lasts shorter than 31 consecutive calendar days. The acting role in the higher function has a temporary character."

      "With students who contribute to the educational function, or who perform activities within the hogeschool in line with their own study program, a fixed-term employment contract can be entered into as a student assistant. Chapters F, M, N and O as well as Articles H-3, H-4, H-5, H-6 and H-8 to H-10 do not apply to the student assistant. The salary for a full-time employment contract as a student assistant is included in Bijlage III of this CAO."

      "Trade Union Contribution Scheme (Article I-8, Bijlage IV)",
      "This scheme allows for the use of gross salary components for the payment of trade union contributions. The full contribution for the relevant calendar year is eligible for the scheme. The scheme applies to employees affiliated with a trade union represented in the SER, or with the RMU or with the CGMW. This includes at least the following organizations: AOb and ABVAKABO FNV, CNV Onderwijs (part of CNV Connectief), UNIENFTO/FvOv, Koninklijke Vereniging van Leraren Lichamelijke Opvoeding (KVLO), Landelijke Beroepsgroep voor Begeleiders in het Onderwijs (LBBO), Nederlandse Vereniging voor Logopedie en Foniatrie (NVLF), Nederlandse Vereniging van Wiskundeleraren (NVvW), Vereniging Leraren Schoolmuziek (VLS), Vereniging Onderwijs Kunst en Cultuur (VONKC), Nederlandse Vereniging voor Onderwijspersoneel (NVOP), Vereniging van schooldecanen en leerlingbegeleiders (NVS-NVL), Vereniging van Leraren in Levende Talen (VLLT), Nederlandse Vereniging voor het Onderwijs in de Natuurwetenschappen (NVON), UNIENFTO. An application can only be submitted to one employer per paid contribution amount. The employee is responsible for the consequences of an incorrect statement. Source: End-of-year bonus. Procedure: The employee must notify the employer before November 1 of the relevant year that they wish to use this trade union contribution scheme. To do so, the employee submits a fully completed application form and proof of payment to the employer. The application form is published on the website of the Vereniging Hogescholen: www.vereniginghogescholen.nl."

      "Supplementary CAO Agreements Job Classification and Appraisal & Remuneration (Bijlage XII)",
      "A. Transitional agreements for hogescholen not ready with job classification by December 21, 2005: An employee classified into a higher function scale due to the new function evaluation system has a retroactive right to this function scale from December 21, 2005. Hogescholen that had not completed the function classification process by December 21, 2005, must set a new completion date, no later than March 31, 2006, after consultation with local trade unions. The elaboration of the plan of approach, including the function structure, must be submitted for approval to local trade unions and the (Central) Medezeggenschapsraad (CMR) in a tripartite consultation. B. Temporary supplementary agreements for hogescholen without an operational appraisal system by January 1, 2006: Hogescholen without an operational appraisal system are bound by Article H-3, paragraph 2, first bullet point: 'a salary increase within the salary scale with the norm percentage as applicable in the function scale'. An employee who believes they perform excellently can submit a motivated request to the manager for twice the norm percentage. If rejected, the manager must provide reasons within 10 working days."

      "Collective Health Insurance (Preambule 7)",
      "Social partners in hbo ensure that pensioners and disabled persons in the sector can also use the collective health insurance or an equivalent provision. Part of the agreements on collective health insurance is that each hogeschool also provides for provisions in the context of work-related care, whether or not in the form of insurance. The costs of this provision or insurance amount to approximately €55 per employee per year."

      "The hogeschool spends 1.41% of the total annual income on decentralized consultation on employment conditions. This budget is available for new agreements to be made by local CAO consultation or for improving existing regulations on, among other things, the following topics: collective disability insurance (if no collective disability insurance is offered in the decentralized consultation, the employer will inform employees in writing about the risk of income loss after the wage-related phase and the possibility of insuring this risk); elder policy."

      "Goals in money: accrual of old-age pension (OP) based on ABP regulations; accrual of life-course salary in the context of the life-course scheme.       "For saved DI-hours that remain after 3 years, alternative application possibilities will be offered according to the choice menu of employment conditions. This includes the possibility to save extra pension in the context of the new pension agreement."

    [
      "Long Special Leave (Article J-14)",
      "The conditions for granting long special leave include a regulation regarding the payment of pension premiums. In accordance with the provisions in the ABP pension agreement, the employer will in all cases recover the specified minimum percentage. Furthermore, the starting point is that for leave solely in the personal interest of the employee, the payment of the entire pension premium, both the employer's and the employee's share, is for the account of the employee, and for leave predominantly in the general interest, the payment of the employer's share is for the account of the employer. Activities predominantly in the general interest may include, among others, activities for development aid, teaching at Edukans, advisory work at Unesco, etc."

      "For the employee who is considered a government employee in the Wet Privatisering ABP, the provisions in the Pension Regulations of the Stichting Pensioenfonds ABP apply regarding the pension provision."

      "Illness and Disability (Article T-2)",
      "For the (former) employee, as referred to in Article T-1, who is wholly or partially prevented from performing work due to illness or disability, the provisions in: a. the ZAHBO; b. the ZW, if the illness commenced after January 1, 2001, and they meet the provisions of the ZW; c. the Pension Regulations of the Stichting Pensioenfonds ABP, apply."

      "In case of full or partial unemployment, the (former) employee can claim a benefit under the WW as well as a supplementary benefit under the BWRHBO if they meet the provisions of these schemes. Notwithstanding paragraph 1, in case of full or partial unemployment arising before January 1, 2001, the (former) employee as referred to in Article T-1 can claim a benefit under the BWRHBO, if they meet the provisions of the BWRHBO."
    ],
    [
      "Collective Health Insurance (Article T-4)",
      "The Vereniging Hogescholen concludes a collective health insurance for all hogescholen. The employee can use this insurance, whereby a specific hbo premium applies for the supplementary insurance. The hogeschool that does not use the collective health insurance provides an offer to its employees consisting of a comparable package under comparable conditions. Regardless of participation in the collective health insurance, the employee receives a gross amount of €300 annually, pro-rata to their employment size. This amount is pensionable."
    ],
    [
      "Rights of Surviving Relatives upon Death (Article T-5)",
      "Upon the death of the employee, the following are eligible for a surviving relatives' benefit in the following order: a. the partner, from whom the deceased was not permanently separated; b. the minor children of the deceased; c. the adult children, parents, brothers or sisters for whom the deceased was the breadwinner. Children within the meaning of the first paragraph of this article also include natural children and children for whom the deceased provided foster parental care. Foster parental care is understood to mean the care for the maintenance and upbringing of the child as if it were one's own child, regardless of any obligation to do so or of receiving compensation for it. The surviving relatives' benefit upon death is equal to the amount formed by multiplying the benefit basis by three and is paid out by the employer as soon as possible, in any case within one month after the death. The income already paid to the employee before their death for a period after their death is deducted from the benefit."
    ],
    [
      "Demotion (Article T-7)",
      "An employee who, from ten years before the pension calculation age in accordance with the ABP Pension Regulations, accepts a new job with a lower salary at the same or another employer affiliated with the ABP, retains the right, upon their own request and with the application of Chapter 3 of the ABP pension regulations, to the pensionable income that applied before accepting the lower salary. The pension premium belonging to the difference between the lower paid function and the old function is divided between employer and employee in the usual manner. Changes in the pension regulations, the premium amount and/or distribution may lead to an adjustment of this CAO article."
    ],
    [
      "WGA-premie (Article T-8)",
      "The employer can partially recover the WGA-vast premium component in the context of the Regeling Werkhervatting Gedeeltelijk Arbeidsgeschikten (WGA) from the employee. This recovery is capped at 50% of the calculation premium in hbo. No more than half of the hogeschool's own premium will ever be withheld."

The scheme can be found at www.vereniginghogescholen.nl. The transitional arrangement for life-course distinguishes between participants who have saved more or less than €3000. The text of the transitional arrangement is published on the website of the Belastingdienst."

    [
      "Seniors Scheme Teaching Staff (SOP) (Bijlage XIII, Hoofdstuk M cao-hbo 2012-2013)",
      "For the employee who uses the M-2 scheme of the CAO-hbo 2012-2013 on January 1, 2015, the scheme remains in force. Article M-2-a Right to reduced working time for older personnel: Unless the interest of the organization opposes it, the working time of an employee who has submitted an application after March 27, 2003, will be reduced while maintaining their employment size, in accordance with one of the following options: a. when they are 55 years or older, a reduction of their working time by 10% of the agreed annual task; b. when they are 57 years or older, a reduction of their working time by 20% of the agreed annual task. The choice for one of the two variants is made once. The employee, in consultation with the employer, determines the effective date of the variant applicable to them within the scheme. The maximum duration of using the SOP scheme is 7 years for a 10% working time reduction, and 5 years for a 20% working time reduction. Employees who started using the SOP scheme before May 1, 2003, in accordance with the provisions applicable in 2002, can, unless the interest of the organization opposes it, make a one-time choice to switch from 12.5% working time reduction to 20% working time reduction from the age of 57 (with a 9% own contribution). To prevent organizational problems, this choice must be communicated to the employer at least 3 calendar months before reaching that age. The employer informs the (involved) employees in a timely manner about this choice. Article M-2-b Termination of employment at the end of the SOP scheme: The employee commits in writing to resign after the period of participation in the SOP scheme as referred to in Article M-2-a, paragraph 2, for at least the extent equal to the working time reduction of the preceding SOP scheme. An employee who is already using the SOP scheme and meets the conditions of the FPU transitional arrangement can extend the SOP period by two or three months so that the FPU scheme can be used immediately thereafter. Article M-2-d Consequences of working time reduction: For the hours by which the employee's working time has been reduced in accordance with Article M-2-a, the employee is deemed to be free from the roster. In case of working time reduction as referred to in Article M-2-a, a deduction is applied to the employee's salary in the cases mentioned in Article M-2-a paragraph 1 under a or b, amounting to: a. for a 10% working time reduction: 4% of the salary that would apply to them without working time reduction under that article; b. for a 20% working time reduction: 9% of the salary that would apply to them without working time reduction under that article. For an employee whose salary is determined according to one of the salary scales 1 to 5, a 10% working time reduction entails an own contribution of 3%, and a 20% working time reduction entails an own contribution of 7%. A reduced working time as referred to in Article M-2-a is not considered incomplete working time for the determination of benefits or allowances under other decisions. All salary-related entitlements are in that case calculated based on the salary that the interested party would have received if they had not used the scheme. For entitlements under the Ziekte en arbeidsongeschiktheidsregeling hbo, the following applies: For an employee who becomes disabled, a waiting period of 13 weeks of illness applies during which the salary is paid, taking into account the reduction under the SOP scheme. After that period, the employee receives 100% of their salary. Agreements can be made with partially disabled employees about continuing participation in the SOP scheme. Article M-2e Additional options: In addition to the basic scheme in Article M-2-a, various alternatives are possible for the employee from May 1, 2003: a. for 7 years, a working time reduction of 12.5% with a deduction of 6% of the salary that would apply to them without working time reduction; b. for 6 years, a working time reduction of 15% with a deduction of 8% of the salary that would apply to them without working time reduction. An employee who has reached the age of 60 and is not yet using one of the variants of the SOP scheme can opt for a 25% working time reduction for five years with a 6% salary deduction. Article M-2-f Anti-accumulation: The employer applies Articles 7 and 8 of the Wet uitkering wegens vervroegd uittreden as they apply from January 1, 1997, mutatis mutandis, regarding the offsetting of extra income from work or business with the employee's salary, with the understanding that the offsetting of extra income cannot lead to a lower amount than the employee would earn if they worked part-time in a position of equal scope. Article M-2-h Transitional arrangement: Demonstrable SOP agreements remain in force if desired, with the understanding that Article M-2-f from the CAO-hbo 2012-2013 does not apply to these employees."
    ]

      "Sustainable Employability Hours (Preambule 4)",
      "Social partners have agreed that as of September 1, 2020, the possibility to save DI-uren ceases to exist. Accumulated savings balances dating from before September 1, 2020, will be spent as much as possible over a period of 3 years on DI-related goals. DI-uren will be processed in the annual task.     ],
    [
      "Normjaartaak (Article A-1)",
      "Annual task of 1659 hours."
    ],
    [
      "Work and Leave (Article J-1)",
      "The total number of vacation hours is calculated on an annual basis, assuming 1659 net working hours per year and the employee's applicable work week and scope. For an employee with a part-time employment, the normjaartaak of 1659 hours is determined proportionally. On non-workdays, the employee is deemed not to perform any work. If a public holiday does not fall on a weekend, this day is compensated by the employee. The employer grants the employee, upon request, the opportunity to take vacation on public holidays and commemorative days that belong to their life conviction."
    ],
    [
      "Actual Weekly Working Hours (Article J-2)",
      "For a full-time employee, the work week consists of the following modalities: 36 hours, 38 hours, or 40 hours. The employee has the right to choose, in consultation with the employer, a work week of 36 hours, 38 hours, or 40 hours, unless compelling business or service interests oppose this choice. This consultation must be concluded at least 3 months before the start of the academic year. Another scope and arrangement of the work week can be agreed upon between employer and employee in consultation. For employees with a part-time employment, this article is applied proportionally to the employment size."
    ],
    [
      "Vacation (Article J-4)",
      "The employee with a work week of: 36 hours is entitled to 219 hours of vacation per calendar year with retention of salary; 38 hours is entitled to 323 hours of vacation per calendar year with retention of salary; 40 hours is entitled to 428 hours of vacation per calendar year with retention of salary. For an employee with a part-time employment, the number of vacation hours is determined proportionally. Portions of hours are rounded up to half hours."
    ],
    [
      "Registration of Vacation (Article J-5)",
      "The leave hours taken by the employee based on Article J-4 are registered by the employer."
    ],
    [
      "Start and Termination of Vacation (Article J-6)",
      "Upon commencing or terminating employment during the calendar year and upon changing the individually agreed weekly working hours, the number of vacation hours to which the employee is entitled is determined proportionally, notwithstanding the provisions of Article J-4."
    ],
    [
      "Vacation and Illness (Article J-7)",
      "An employee who does not perform the agreed work due to illness accrues vacation hours over the statutory minimum number of vacation hours, namely four times the agreed weekly working hours. For the above-statutory part of the vacation hours, these are accrued over the first 6 months of sick leave. Periods are aggregated if they follow each other with an interruption of less than one month. After that period, an employee who partially does not perform the work due to illness accrues leave rights proportionally to the hours worked. If the employee falls ill during their vacation, the leave is converted into sick leave."
    ],
    [
      "Taking Vacation (Article J-8)",
      "The employer grants the employee vacation upon their request, unless compelling reasons oppose it. If this is the case, the employer notifies the employee in writing with reasons within two weeks. Employer and employee ensure that sufficient opportunity is provided or taken to take the vacation entitlements in the relevant year. Vacation that has not been taken due to illness, or that has not been taken because service interests prevented it, can be carried over by the employee to the following year. The employer can, after obtaining the consent of the PMR, designate a maximum of one week and four non-consecutive days per year as compulsory vacation. In consultation with the PMR, the employer can designate longer periods than those mentioned in the previous paragraph as compulsory vacation. The consent of the PMR is required for this. The PMR can make compensation agreements."
    ],
    [
      "Short Special Leave (Article J-9)",
      "The employer grants the employee short special leave with retention of income, subject to the provisions of the second paragraph of this article, insofar as their work coincides with one or more of the following circumstances: a. the exercise of voting rights, compliance with a legal obligation, or taking a state-administered or recognized examination or test, insofar as this cannot be done in free time and a change of duty is not possible; b. attending meetings or sessions of or performing work for public law bodies in which the employee has been appointed or elected, insofar as this cannot be done in free time; c. exercising membership of a state-appointed or recognized examination committee or acting as a state commissioner for an examination, for a total of a maximum of 14 days per year to be determined in consultation with the employer, insofar as the employer is compensated for this; d. death of the partner, parents or children, including step-, in-law or foster family members of the employee or their partner, for 4 days; of blood relatives or relatives by marriage of the employee or their partner in the second degree, for 2 days; if the employee in the latter case is responsible for arranging the funeral or the estate, leave is granted for a maximum of 4 days; e. relocation in case of a change of standplaats: for 2, in special cases a maximum of 4 days; f. searching for a home in case of a change of standplaats, for a maximum of 2 days; g. civil or church marriage or registration of the employee's partnership, for a total of 2 days, insofar as the wedding day(s) or partnership registration day(s) fall within this period; h. marriage or partnership registration of blood relatives or relatives by marriage of the first or second degree of the employee or their partner, for 1 day; i. attending a statutory member meeting of the central organization referred to in Article V-7, insofar as this cannot be done in free time; j. complying with a request to be heard as a witness or expert in legal proceedings. If the circumstance mentioned in the first paragraph, under b of this article, occurs and the employee receives a fixed allowance in connection with the activities for which leave is granted, a deduction is applied to their income for the time they enjoy the leave. This deduction does not exceed what the employee can be deemed to receive as a fixed allowance for the activities performed during the time corresponding to the leave. The special leave, referred to in this article, must be requested from the employer at least one week in advance, or, in unforeseen cases, as soon as possible. The employer can, in appropriate cases, nevertheless consider the employee's absence as special leave with retention of income if the employee has not complied with this requirement. If the employee wishes to use unpaid leave or a leave day in the following cases, the employer will provide them with the opportunity to do so: engagement of the employee; death of blood relatives or relatives by marriage of the employee or their partner, in the third and fourth degree; relocation other than in case of a change of standplaats; the 25-, 40- and 50-year service or marriage anniversary or anniversary of the registration of the employee's partnership and the 25-, 40-, 50- and 60-year marriage anniversary or anniversary of the registration of the partnership of the parents, including step-, in-law or foster parents, of the employee or their partner; youth and youth work. Notwithstanding the provisions of the third paragraph of this article, the special leave referred to in the first paragraph under i must be requested from the employer at least two months in advance."
    ],
    [
      "Maternity and Childbirth Leave and Parental Leave (Article J-10)",
      "The female employee is entitled to paid pregnancy and childbirth leave in connection with her delivery. The provisions of the Wet Arbeid en Zorg apply. The right to pregnancy leave exists from six weeks before the day after the presumed date of delivery, as indicated in a written statement from a doctor or midwife submitted to the employer, up to and including the day of delivery. Pregnancy leave starts no later than four weeks before the day after the presumed date of delivery. Childbirth leave starts on the day after delivery and amounts to ten consecutive weeks or as many more as the number of days by which the pregnancy leave was less than six weeks. For the application of the third paragraph, days for which the female employee received sick pay under Article 29a, second paragraph, of the Ziektewet during the period she is entitled to pregnancy leave, but that leave has not yet started, are considered days for which she received pregnancy leave. The employee is entitled to unpaid parental leave in accordance with the Wet Arbeid en Zorg."
    ],
    [
      "Care Leave (Article J-11)",
      "The employer grants the employee paid care leave in case of illness and necessary nursing of the partner, parents or children, including step-, in-law or foster family members of the employee or their partner, for a maximum of twice the weekly working hours per year. If, according to a medical certificate to be submitted to the employer, the continuous presence of the employee with the sick person is necessary for a longer period, this period can be extended."
    ],
    [
      "Calamity Leave and Other Short-term Absence Leave (Article J-12)",
      "The employee is entitled to calamity leave under Article 4:1 of the WAZO, in case of the circumstances mentioned in that article. For the delivery of the spouse, registered partner, the person with whom the employee cohabits unmarried, or the person whose child the employee acknowledges, the employee is entitled to paid leave for the duration of once the weekly working hours, for a period of four weeks, calculated from the moment the child actually lives at the same address. These days do not have to be taken consecutively. As of July 1, 2020, the employee is entitled to additional birth leave, as referred to in Article 4:2a WAZO."
    ],
    [
      "Adoption Leave (Article J-13)",
      "Paid leave in accordance with the WAZO is granted for a maximum of six weeks in case of adoption of a child. The salary payment during the leave amounts to 100%. The leave starts from four weeks before the first day of actual placement of the child and does not have to be taken consecutively, but at least within a period of 26 weeks. The employee submits a document to the employer showing that the child is or will be adopted. The employee can request the employer to spread the leave over a period of 26 weeks. The employer generally grants the request to take the leave spread out, unless compelling business or service interests oppose it. If, as a result of an adoption request, two or more children are actually placed for adoption simultaneously, the right to leave only exists for one of those children. During the leave, the employee is entitled to a benefit from the UWV, to be applied for by the employer. The employer supplements this benefit to 100% of their salary."
    ],
    [
      "Long Special Leave (Article J-14)",
      "The employer can grant an employee, upon their request, long special leave for all or part of their work activities. The employer can attach conditions to the granting of the leave."
    ],
    [
      "Long Special Leave for Political Functions (Article J-15)",
      "An employee who accepts: a. membership of the Tweede Kamer der Staten-Generaal, b. the function of member of Gedeputeerde Staten of a province, or c. the function of substituut-ombudsman, automatically receives long special leave, without retention of income. The employer grants an employee who accepts the function of wethouder, upon their request, long special leave for all or part of their work activities without retention of income. Insofar as the exercise of the function at the employer is harmed, the employer can refuse such leave on its own initiative, provided the employee has been heard beforehand. As of December 1, 2014, the period for which leave is granted in connection with fulfilling political functions can last a maximum of nine years from the aforementioned date."
    ],
    [
      "Work Time Reduction for Seniors (Article M-2)",
      "Article M-2-a General: An employee who reaches the AOW-gerechtigde leeftijd minus ten years, with an employment contract of 0.4 FTE and higher and five consecutive years of service in hbo, can, from a moment to be determined by themselves (unless the interest of the organization opposes it), choose once for the possibility to: reduce working time by a fixed percentage of a maximum of 20% of the agreed annual task in recognizable half-days; for a period of a maximum of five years. The employee commits in writing to resign after the period of a maximum of five years referred to in paragraph 1 for an extent that is at least equal to the working time reduction. The employee pays an own contribution over the monthly salary belonging to the working time reduction referred to in paragraph 1 of: 45% in the period between ten and five years before the AOW-gerechtigde leeftijd; 25% in the period between five and zero years before the AOW-gerechtigde leeftijd. Notwithstanding the mentioned percentages in paragraph 3, for employees in scales 1 to 7, an own contribution over the monthly salary of: 35% in the period between ten and five years before the AOW-gerechtigde leeftijd; 20% in the period between five and zero years before the AOW-gerechtigde leeftijd. During illness in the second year of illness, the employee's own contribution percentage is set to zero. A reduced working time as referred to in paragraphs 2 and 3 is not considered incomplete working time for the determination of benefits or allowances under other decisions. All entitlements related to the monthly salary and employment size are in that case calculated based on the monthly salary that the employee would have received if they had not used the scheme. An employee who uses the Work Time Reduction for Seniors scheme cannot claim extra DI-uren in accordance with Article M-1-c. An employee who uses the Work Time Reduction for Seniors scheme is not permitted to generate new income from work or business during the time by which the agreed annual task has been reduced. Article M-2-d Transitional Arrangement: An employee who has fully used the right under Article M-2 of the CAO-hbo 2012-2013 cannot claim the Work Time Reduction for Seniors scheme. The extra DI-uren in accordance with Article M-1-c do not apply to an employee who uses Article M-2 of the CAO-hbo 2012-2013. For an employee who used Article M-2 of the CAO-hbo 2012-2013 and has switched to the Work Time Reduction for Seniors scheme, at least the percentage by which the employment would be terminated after the M-2 scheme of the CAO-hbo 2012-2013 remains intact."
    ],

      "Leave for Consultation and Advisory Activities (Article V-7)",
      "The employer grants the employee, if there is reason to do so according to further rules to be given by the Minister of Education, Culture and Science or the Minister of Economic Affairs and Climate, upon request, short or long special leave from all or part of their work activities for: a. performing work of a legal status nature in or for committees for organized consultation as referred to in Title IV of the Rechtspositiebesluit onderwijspersoneel; b. performing work for a central organization of civil servants' associations or an association affiliated with such a central organization, represented in the Centrale Commissie van Georganiseerd Overleg in Ambtenarenzaken, or Raad voor Overheidspersoneelsbeleid, of which they are a member; c. participating as a course participant in a course at the invitation of a central organization or association as referred to under b, for a maximum of five days per year insofar as the employee is a cadre member of the relevant central organization or association, or a maximum of six days per two years insofar as the employee is a member of the relevant central organization or association, if and insofar as a change of duty is not possible. If long special leave is granted under the first or second paragraph of this article, the provisions of Article Q-5 regarding its termination apply mutatis mutandis."

  "termination_information": [
    [
      "Use of Employment Contract Model D-3 (Preambule 3)",
      "The CAO articles relating to employment contracts – particularly in Hoofdstuk D – have been revised, with the guiding principles: The nature of the job offer determines the form of the employment contract; Structural work (continuity in the job offer) is performed on the basis of an employment contract for an indefinite period, or on the basis of an employment contract for a definite period with a view to permanent employment; in doing so, the limits for permanent and flexible work, agreed with the co-determination body, will be respected. Hogescholen endorse the guiding principle that structural work should be performed on the basis of a permanent contract or a temporary contract with a view to permanent employment. The aim is to promote as much as possible the movement from temporary to permanent contracts. The number of realized D-3 contracts is important for monitoring the percentage of non-permanent contracts at sector level. The agreements with the co-determination body on the percentage of flexible (temporary and hired) contracts are decisive for the percentage of D-3 contracts at hogeschool level. In other words, the individual hogeschool will not be held accountable for not offering D-3 contracts as long as this fits within the percentage agreed with the medezeggenschapsraad. Temporariness in employment contracts, and thus flexibility, must be reasoned and explainable. Social partners aim to: Prevent flexible labor in the sense of unreasoned temporariness; Bring as many employment relationships as possible under the regime of the CAO in various variants; Limit uncertainty for the employee within the employment relationship where possible, such as avoiding 'revolving door' constructions; Reward equal work as equally as possible. Temporary agency work should be limited to absorbing peaks in the job offer and to replacement in case of illness. These agreements should lead to a decrease in the percentage of non-permanent contracts at sector level. During the term of the CAO, social partners will monitor whether this objective has been achieved. Should it appear on January 1, 2022, that the introduction of the new Article D-3 does not lead to a decrease in the percentage of non-permanent contracts, social partners will revert to the CAO texts of Hoofdstuk D of the CAO-hbo 2017-2018. Forms of temporariness that are in principle explainable: Work field expertise that adds value to the study program (such as top musicians, medical specialists, lawyers, accountants); Predictable end of funding (lectoraat, research based on subsidy money), temporary care obligation to be fulfilled during the phasing out of a study program that the hogeschool is legally obliged to maintain for another five years; Peak and illness: temporary replacement in case of illness or pregnancy. Engaging extra capacity in circumstances of a clearly temporary nature and above-average high job offer; Business risk: absorbing more than normal entrepreneurial risk, e.g., to absorb a decrease in student intake; Hiring external specialists in the context of business operations (legal, financial, ICT). The hogeschool's budget states what percentage of flexibility is needed and which contract forms (temporary, temporary agency work, ZZP) will be used. In addition, a percentage of flexibility is set as an upper limit, depending on the hogeschool's profile. Within this framework, the maximum size of the buffer for risk at the hogeschool level is determined, in agreement with the PMR. If a crisis or calamity arises in the budget year as a result of which the upper limit is exceeded, an adjustment can be made in the interim - in consultation with the PMR. Accountability is provided in the annual report and in the annual accounts. The effect of this procedural approach is that: Prior control is made possible by the co-determination body with local knowledge of the hogeschool; Adjustment and monitoring during the budget year is possible; At the end of the budget year, accountability is provided for the final realization."
    ],
    [
      "Dismissal Committee (Preambule 7)",
      "Social partners established a dismissal committee for the hbo sector in 2016 for handling dismissal requests due to business economic reasons. The committee was established for a limited period. During this period, social partners will evaluate the experiences gained with the dismissal committee and compare them with the experiences with the dismissal procedure at the UWV in other public sectors. The advantages and disadvantages of both routes and the costs will be taken into account in the evaluation. Social partners aim to make a well-considered choice by December 2020 at the latest as to whether or not the dismissal committee will be continued after December 2020."
